# Cross-package hook requests

This file is **append-only**. A work package that needs a change in a path it does not own appends a request here, codes against an interface or stub in its own package, and carries on. The owning WP (or the integrator between waves) implements the request and marks it done by appending a line below it. Do not edit or delete other entries.

Format:

```
## <requesting WP> -> <owner path>: <exact request>
<optional detail, rationale, the contract to code against>
```

---

## WP0-contract -> internal/config/validate.go: accept and normalise the network config
- Accept `model.ModeAggregator` in the `mode` enum (`model.ModeFeeder` is for `airview-feeder` only and stays invalid for the main app). Today `Validate` resets "aggregator" to standalone.
- Call `notes = append(notes, out.Aggregator.Normalize()...)` and `notes = append(notes, model.NormalizeUplinks(out.Uplinks)...)`. Both clamp to documented ranges, are idempotent, and return notes in the existing "x is out of range; y is used" style. `Aggregator.Normalize` also turns a zero-valued block (a document without an `aggregator` key decoded into a zero `Config`) back into `model.DefaultAggregator()`.
- Feeds: validate the new `Feed.ClockClass` with `model.ParseClockClass` ("" = detect) and keep `Feed.NoForward`. `model.FeedAuto` is valid only in `FeederConfig`, not in `Config.Feeds`.
- An uplink's `Inputs` should be checked against `Config.Feeds[].ID` (unknown IDs dropped with a note).

## WP0-contract -> internal/config/holder.go: deep-copy the new slices in Clone
`Clone` must also do `out.Uplinks = model.CloneUplinks(cfg.Uplinks)` and `out.Aggregator = cfg.Aggregator.Clone()`. Otherwise `Holder.Get()` copies share `Uplinks[].Inputs`, `Aggregator.TrustedProxies`, `TrustedRelays` and `Limits.AllowCIDRs` backing arrays.

## WP0-contract -> internal/config/secrets.go: redact and merge the new secrets
Treat like `hub.password` in `Redact`, `RedactPublic` and `MergeSecrets`:
- `uplinks[i].enrolToken` (matched by uplink `id` between incoming and current);
- `aggregator.outputToken`;
- `aggregator.tlsKey` (a file path, but it reveals where the key lives; `RedactPublic` at least).

## WP0-contract -> internal/config/config.go: list handling in parse()
Add `if z.Uplinks != nil { cfg.Uplinks = z.Uplinks }` next to the Feeds/Antennas handling. (Uplink elements already decode on top of `model.DefaultUplink()` through `Uplink.UnmarshalJSON`, so missing fields get their defaults.)

## WP0-contract -> internal/server/api_core.go: keep unsent sections in PUT /api/config
`apiPutConfig` keeps stored values only for the keys it lists. Add `if !has("uplinks") { in.Uplinks = cur.Uplinks }` and `if !has("aggregator") { in.Aggregator = cur.Aggregator }`. Without this, an old UI that does not send these keys would clear the uplinks and reset the aggregator block. (`Aggregator.Normalize` restores a zero block to defaults, but that would still lose custom aggregator settings.)

## WP0-contract -> all WPs: StationInfo is named StationMeta
DESIGN Â§6.1's `StationInfo{Idx, SID, Name, ClockCls, Located}` is `model.StationMeta`, because `model.StationInfo` already exists as the effective-station-position DTO of `Status.station` (used by `internal/engine`). `StationResolver.Info` returns `StationMeta`. `RawFrame.ClockCls` and `StationMeta.ClockCls` are typed `model.ClockClass` (underlying `uint8`), not bare `uint8`.

## WP0-contract -> internal/feed (WP-F): use the shared clock contract
Expose the detected class as `model.ClockClass` (`ClockGPSUTC`/`ClockGPSGPST`/`ClockGPSEmul`/`ClockHW12`/`ClockSDR12`/â€¦), produce ticks with `model.GPSTicks` (GPS classes) and `model.Unwrap48` / `model.Unwrap24`, report epoch changes with `model.ClockEventReason`, and fill `RawFrame.{Epoch, ClockCls, Ticks, HostNs}`. The legacy `GPSNs` / `Clock12MHz` fields stay as they are for standalone consumers. `Feed.Forwardable()` implements the no-forward rule of DESIGN Â§1.

## WP0-contract -> internal/tracker (WP-T): MLAT display fields
When a `DecodedUpdate` has `Src == model.SrcMLATAV` (or `SrcMLATExt`), fill `Aircraft.MLATGrade` (`model.GradeString(u.Grade)`, only when `model.Displayable`) and `Aircraft.MLATSigmaM` from `u.SigmaM`. `Aircraft.Mine` / `Aircraft.Stations` are set by the per-viewer projection (owner: own SIDs only; admin: all; public: never).

## WP-F-feed -> internal/model/config.go: exclude demo and hub feeds in Feed.Forwardable
`feed.Manager.Forwardable(feedID)` (the per-frame check for the uplink tee) already refuses feeds of type `demo` (synthetic simulator data) and `hub` (frames received by another station, which would be attributed to this one), besides `MLATOnly` and `NoForward`. Please make `model.Feed.Forwardable()` agree: `return !f.MLATOnly && !f.NoForward && f.Type != FeedDemo && f.Type != FeedHub`, so config validation / UI and the uplink use one rule. Until then use `feed.Manager.Forwardable`.

## WP-F-feed -> docs/packages/feed.md: document the WP-F API
New public API of `internal/feed` (contract in `internal/feed/WIRING.md`): `BeastParser.OnEscape`, `BeastParser.Unknown`, escape constants `BeastReceiverID`/`BeastUUID`/`BeastPing`/`BeastOption`/`BeastUUIDLen`/`BeastOptPing`/`BeastOptSlowDown`, `IsBeastEscapeType`, `AppendBeastEscape`/`AppendBeastPing`/`AppendBeastOption`/`AppendBeastUUID`/`AppendBeastReceiverID`, `ParseBeastPing`/`ParseBeastReceiverID`/`ParseBeastUUID`/`NormalizeUUID`, `BeastPingValue`/`BeastPingRTT`; `ClockTracker`, `ClockEvent`, `ClockDetail*`, `DefaultBackwardsTolerance`, `DefaultStepConfirmFrames`; `Timestamper.{OnClockEvent, Class, DetectedClass, SetClassOverride, ClassOverride, SetEpochBase, Epoch, LastTicks, Clock, Stamp}`, `GPSRegainStatuses`, `OverrideApplies`; `Manager.{OnClockEvent, Clock, Forwardable}`, `ForwardableFrame`, `ClockState`, `Session.{Clock, Forwardable, ReportClockEvent}`. Beast/AVR frames now fill `RawFrame.{HostNs, ClockCls, Epoch, Ticks}`.

## WP-A-auth -> internal/registry (WP-R): persistent account and session stores
Implement `auth.AccountStore` and `auth.SessionStore` (`internal/auth/account.go`, `internal/auth/session.go`) on `stations.db`, so aggregator logins survive restarts. Store `Session.ID` (SHA-256 of the token), never a raw token; no IP columns (DESIGN Â§8.5). `Account.RecoveryHashes` can be a JSON column. `ByUsername` receives already-normalised names. `Touch` may coalesce writes (the service already calls it at most once a minute per session). `MemoryAccounts` / `MemorySessions` are the reference behaviour; `internal/auth/service_test.go` can be re-run against the SQLite stores.

## WP-A-auth -> internal/engine, cmd/airview (WP-E): wire the account service and admin bootstrap
- Build `svc := auth.NewService(accounts, sessions, auth.Options{Issuer: cfg.Aggregator.Name})` over the registry stores, call `server.SetAccountService(svc)` (exists in `internal/server/auth_net.go`) and run `go svc.RunJanitor(ctx, 10*time.Minute)`.
- DESIGN Â§7 "startup refuses aggregator mode until an admin password and TOTP exist": at start in aggregator mode, if `!svc.AdminReady(ctx)`, print `svc.MintBootstrapToken(ctx)` to the console/journal (stdout only, not slog files) together with the URL of the setup page, and keep the feeder listeners closed until `AdminReady` becomes true. The browser flow is `GET /api/auth/setup`, `POST /api/auth/setup/begin`, `POST /api/auth/setup/finish` (internal/server/auth_routes.go).
- CLI (Â§20.1): `airview admin init` = `svc.InitAdmin(user, pw)` -> show secret/URI -> read a code -> `svc.ConfirmAdminTOTP` -> print recovery codes. `airview admin reset --data <dir>` = `svc.ResetAdmin(user, newPW)` + `ConfirmAdminTOTP` (break-glass, DESIGN Â§7).

## WP-A-auth -> internal/server (WP-H): register the account routes and use the proxy-aware client IP
- Append `AuthRoutes()` (internal/server/auth_routes.go) to `routes` (e.g. in an `init` like routes_ext.go), regenerate docs/API.md, and in `TestRouteTable` exempt `/api/auth/*` from "mutating route is not admin" (those handlers check the session scope themselves and return 404 in standalone mode). Add `specialTests` entries pointing at `TestAggregatorOwnerScope` / `TestAggregatorBootstrapAPI` (internal/server/auth_net_test.go).
- docs/API.md: `sessionInfo` gained optional fields `csrfToken`, `aggregator`, `scope`, `username`, `totp`, `codeRequired`, `setupRequired`, `recoveryLeft`; `POST /api/login` in aggregator mode takes `{"username","password","code"}` and sets `SameSite=Strict` (+`Secure` over TLS). Please regenerate.
- Replace `clientIP(r)` by `s.remoteIP(r)` in sse.go (`c.ip`) and for any rate limit / feed-check IP match; in aggregator mode do not log raw IPs (api_core.go factory reset and DB maintenance logs). `isLoopback(r)` now also returns false for proxied requests.
- Scoped routes: use `s.viewer(r)` (model.Viewer for cache keys) and `s.requireScope(w, r, model.ScopeOwner|ScopeAdmin)` from internal/server/auth_routes.go.

## WP-A-auth -> web/static (WP-W): CSRF header and aggregator login
Send `X-CSRF-Token: <csrfToken>` (from the login reply or `GET /api/session`) on every non-GET request; harmless in standalone (checked only when sent), required in aggregator mode. Aggregator login: user name + password; when the 401 reply has `codeRequired: true` ask for the authenticator or recovery code and resend all three fields. Pages: register (`POST /api/auth/register`), TOTP enrol (`/api/auth/totp/begin|confirm|disable`, show `uri` as QR and `secret` for manual entry), recovery codes (shown once), password change, sessions list / log out others, first-run setup (`/api/auth/setup*`). When `recoveryLeft` is present after a login, warn the user.

## WP-A-auth -> internal/model (integrator, model frozen): file-configurable auth policy (Â§20.3)
Optional `aggregator.auth` block so session lifetimes are configurable without code: `{ownerSessionDays: 30, ownerIdleDays: 14, adminSessionHours: 12, adminIdleMinutes: 120, registration: "open"|"closed" (default "open"), loginFreeFailures: 5}`. `internal/auth.Options.Policy` already takes these values; until the block exists the defaults in `auth.DefaultPolicy()` apply.

## WP-CF-configfiles -> cmd/airview (WP-E): wire the config subcommand and file/env flags
- Before `platformInit`/instance lock: `if cli.IsConfigCommand(os.Args[1:]) { os.Exit(cli.RunConfig(os.Args[2:], cli.OSEnv())) }` (package `airview/internal/cli`). Treat it like `-version` for console attachment on Windows GUI builds.
- New flags: `-config <file>` (operator JSONC file) and repeatable `-set path=value`; pass them to the engine (see the engine request below) as `config.LoadOptions{Dir, File, Environ: os.Environ(), Sets}`.
- `-data` should default to `$AIRVIEW_DATA` when the flag is empty (`config.EnvDataDir`), before `config.ResolveDataDir`.
- When `-config`/`$AIRVIEW_CONFIG` names a file that is missing or not valid JSONC, a headless start should fail with the error (exit 1) instead of running without it.
- Usage text: mention `airview config check|print|example|env`.

## WP-CF-configfiles -> internal/engine: load and save through config layers
- `Options` gains `ConfigFile string` and `ConfigSets []string`. In `Start` replace `config.LoadWithWarnings(e.dir)` by `cfg, layers, warns, err := config.LoadLayered(config.LoadOptions{Dir: e.dir, File: opts.ConfigFile, Environ: os.Environ(), Sets: opts.ConfigSets})` and keep `layers`.
- `ApplyConfig` and `mutateConfig`: replace `config.Save(e.dir, next)` + `holder.Set(next)` by `eff, err := e.layers.Commit(e.dir, next)` + `holder.Set(eff)` (Commit keeps operator/env/-set values out of config.json and re-applies them, so locked settings stay in force and env secrets never reach disk). Compare `config.Equal(cur, eff)` for the no-op check.
- `FactoryReset`: `cfg, err := e.layers.FactoryReset(e.dir)`.
- Expose `Layers()` (or `Overrides() []config.Override` / `Locked(path) bool`) for the server. A nil `*config.Layers` behaves exactly like today.

## WP-CF-configfiles -> internal/server: show locked settings
- `GET /api/config` (admin): add `"locked": [{"path","origin"}]` from `engine.Layers().Overrides()` (origin of env/file only, never values) so the UI can disable those inputs and say "set by the operator file / AIRVIEW_X". Optional `GET /api/config/check` returning `config.Check` issues for the admin.

## WP-CF-configfiles -> deploy/** (Linux installer, Docker): ship the example config
- Install `internal/config/airview.example.jsonc` (or the output of `airview config example`) as `/etc/airview/airview.example.jsonc`; create `/etc/airview/airview.jsonc` (mode 0640, owner root:airview) only when absent; systemd unit `Environment=AIRVIEW_CONFIG=/etc/airview/airview.jsonc AIRVIEW_DATA=/var/lib/airview`; run `airview config check` in `ExecStartPre=` and in the upgrade script. Docker: document `AIRVIEW_*` variables and `*_FILE` secrets (docs/CONFIG.md).

## WP-CF-configfiles: done -> the four WP0-contract requests to internal/config (validate.go mode/Normalize/clockClass/inputs, holder.go Clone, secrets.go redaction+merge, config.go uplinks list handling) are implemented and tested.


## WP-G-geo -> internal/latency: re-import the geometry from internal/mlat/geo (DESIGN Â§9.1)
DESIGN Â§9.1 moves `ecef` and `propagationNs` out of `internal/latency/timebase.go` into `airview/internal/mlat/geo`; latency should re-import them:
- `ecef(lat, lon, h)` -> `geo.ToECEF(lat, lon, h)` (returns `geo.Vec3{X,Y,Z}`; identical formula and WGS-84 constants).
- `propagationNs(distM, h1, h2)` -> `atm.PropagationNsDist(distM, h1, h2)` with `atm := geo.DefaultAtmosphere()` held once. Note the model differences: H = 7000 m per DESIGN (latency used 7350 m) and the new path model is checked against a numeric ray trace to ~1 mm at 400 km (the old one was a constant-curvature 4/3-earth approximation). Measured against the old function: -6.2 ns at 400 km / -5.0 ns at 300 km / -1.1 ns at 150 km (low receiver, aircraft at 3-11 km); with H = 7350 the difference is only -1.5 / -0.9 / 0.0 ns, i.e. most of it is the DESIGN's H = 7 km. Re-baseline `reference_test.go` tolerances if they are tighter. Where both ECEF ends are known, `atm.PropagationNs(p1, p2)` is the exact form. If latency wants the old H, `geo.Atmosphere{N0: 315, H: 7350, K: 1}` reproduces its profile.

## WP-G-geo -> internal/model (integrator, model frozen) + internal/config: file-configurable propagation/geoid settings (Â§20.3)
Optional fields in `AggMLAT` (JSON under `aggregator.mlat`), consumed by the MLAT engine through `geo.Atmosphere{N0, H, K}.Normalize()` and `geoid.Load`/`geoid.SetDefault`:
- `refractivityN0` (float, default 315, valid 200..450; 0 = use METAR-derived N0 when available, else 315),
- `refractivityScaleHeightM` (float, default 7000, valid 4000..12000),
- `geoidFile` (string path, default "" = embedded EGM96 1Â° grid; accepts NGA WW15MGH.GRD, GeographicLib .pgm or AirView .avg; a finer grid for regions with steep geoid).
k_atm is estimated online (mlat/quality, bounded by `geo.KAtmMin..KAtmMax`), so it needs no setting. Until these exist the engine uses `geo.DefaultAtmosphere()` and the embedded grid.

## Integrator (after Wave 0): status of every open request

- WP0-contract -> internal/config/validate.go, holder.go, secrets.go, config.go: **DONE** (by WP-CF-configfiles, see its line above; checked by the integrator, tests pass).
- WP0-contract -> internal/server/api_core.go (keep unsent `uplinks` / `aggregator` in PUT /api/config): **DONE** (integrator).
- WP0-contract -> all WPs (StationInfo is named StationMeta): **DONE** (informational; no code uses the old name).
- WP0-contract -> internal/feed (shared clock contract): **DONE** (by WP-F-feed: RawFrame.{HostNs, ClockCls, Epoch, Ticks}, model.ClockClass, ClockEventReason, Manager.Forwardable).
- WP0-contract -> internal/tracker (MLAT display fields): **DONE** (integrator). Updates with `Src` = `mlat-av`/`mlat-ext` are MLAT positions. `Aircraft.mlatGrade` (only when displayable) and `mlatSigmaM` are shown while that MLAT fix is the current position (test `TestNetworkMLATGradeAndSigma`). `Mine`/`Stations` stay with the per-viewer projection, which does not exist yet.
- WP-F-feed -> internal/model/config.go (Feed.Forwardable excludes demo and hub): **DONE** (integrator).
- WP-F-feed -> docs/packages/feed.md (document the WP-F API): **DONE** (integrator; "Network additions" section).
- WP-A-auth -> internal/registry (WP-R) persistent account/session stores: **REJECTED for this wave: internal/registry does not exist yet.** Carried over to WP-R. Until then cmd/airview uses the in-memory stores: accounts are lost on restart, and the app logs a warning about it.
- WP-A-auth -> internal/engine, cmd/airview (account service and admin bootstrap): **PARTLY DONE** (integrator). In aggregator mode, cmd/airview/accounts.go builds the service with the `aggregator.auth` policy, installs it (`SetAccountService`), runs the janitor, and prints the one-time setup token on stdout only (checked in a live run: the token is not in airview.log). **Open:**
  - Keeping the feeder listeners closed until `AdminReady`: no feeder listener exists yet. The WP that opens one must check `AdminReady` first.
  - `airview admin init` / `airview admin reset --data`: **REJECTED for now.** With in-memory stores a separate CLI process cannot reach the server's accounts. Implement them together with WP-R's persistent stores.
- WP-A-auth -> internal/server (WP-H) routes and proxy-aware IP: **DONE** (integrator).
  - `authRoutes` are appended to the route table in `init`; TestRouteTable exempts `/api/auth/*`; specialTests entries added.
  - docs/API.md regenerated, including the login notes for aggregator mode.
  - sse.go uses `s.remoteIP`. The factory-reset and DB-maintenance logs use `s.logClient`, which logs no raw IPs in aggregator mode.
- WP-A-auth -> web/static (WP-W) CSRF header and aggregator login: **CSRF part DONE** (integrator). api.js sends `X-CSRF-Token` on every non-GET request; the token comes from /api/session, /api/login, /api/logout and /api/auth/register. **Open for WP-W:**
  - aggregator login (user name + code), register, TOTP enrol, recovery codes, password change, sessions, first-run setup pages
  - the `recoveryLeft` warning
  - disabling the inputs listed under `locked` in GET /api/config
- WP-A-auth -> internal/model (aggregator.auth policy): **DONE** (integrator). `model.AggAuth` under `aggregator.auth` has these fields; docs regenerated with `go generate ./internal/config`:
  - ownerSessionDays 30 (1..365)
  - ownerIdleDays 14 (1..365)
  - adminSessionHours 12 (1..168)
  - adminIdleMinutes 120 (5..1440)
  - registration "open"|"closed"
  - loginFreeFailures 5 (1..100)
- WP-CF-configfiles -> cmd/airview: **DONE** (integrator):
  - `airview config ...` dispatch (console attached on Windows)
  - `-config` and repeatable `-set`
  - `-data` defaults to `$AIRVIEW_DATA`
  - a headless start exits with status 1 when an explicitly named operator file is missing or invalid
  - usage text
- WP-CF-configfiles -> internal/engine: **DONE** (integrator; test `TestConfigLayers`):
  - `Options.{ConfigFile, ConfigSets, Environ}`. A nil Environ means no environment overrides; cmd passes os.Environ().
  - `config.LoadLayered` at start.
  - `Layers.Commit` in ApplyConfig/mutateConfig, `Layers.FactoryReset`.
  - `Engine.Layers()`.
- WP-CF-configfiles -> internal/server (locked settings): **DONE** (integrator). Admin `GET /api/config` gets `"locked":[{"path","origin"}]` only when overrides exist (test `TestConfigLockedSettings`). The optional `GET /api/config/check` is **not done**; `airview config check` covers it.
- WP-CF-configfiles -> deploy/**: **DONE for deploy/pi** (integrator):
  - The installer writes `/etc/airview/airview.example.jsonc` (from `airview config example`), creates `/etc/airview/airview.jsonc` once (0640 root:airview), and runs `airview config check` on install and upgrade.
  - The unit sets `AIRVIEW_CONFIG` / `AIRVIEW_DATA` and runs `config check -q` in `ExecStartPre=-`. That check is non-fatal: the app repairs a damaged config.json itself, and an unusable operator file stops the start anyway.
  - **Open:** no Docker packaging or generic x86-64 VPS installer yet. The env and `_FILE` secrets are documented in docs/CONFIG.md.
- WP-G-geo -> internal/latency (re-import the geometry): **DONE** (integrator):
  - `ecef` wraps `mgeo.ToECEF`, and `propagationNs` uses `mgeo.DefaultAtmosphere().PropagationNsDist`. The old 4/3-earth ray code is removed.
  - `reference_test.go` now uses H = 7000 m for its reference atmosphere. The difference to the model is now at most 2.9 ns at 450-500 km; the 5 ns tolerance is unchanged.
- WP-G-geo -> internal/model + internal/config (propagation/geoid settings): **DONE** (integrator). New settings, with no consumer yet (the MLAT engine is still to come):
  - `aggregator.mlat.refractivityN0`: default 315, 200..450, 0 = from METAR
  - `refractivityScaleHeightM`: default 7000, 4000..12000
  - `geoidFile`: "" = embedded grid

## WP-R-registry -> internal/engine, cmd/airview (WP-E): use the persistent registry in aggregator mode
- Open `reg, err := registry.OpenDir(dataDir, registry.Options{Policy: registry.PolicyFromConfig(cfg.Aggregator), Logger: log})` in aggregator mode only (standalone never opens stations.db), `go reg.Run(ctx)`, `defer reg.Close()`.
- Replace the in-memory stores in cmd/airview/accounts.go: `auth.NewService(reg.Accounts(), reg.Sessions(), auth.Options{...})`. Drop the "accounts are lost on restart" warning.
- On every config apply: `reg.SetPolicy(registry.PolicyFromConfig(next.Aggregator))` (admission, defaultPin, limits.maxStations, retention).
- Host station: `reg.EnsureHost(ctx, name, adminAccountID)` returns idx 1 (`model.HostStation`); set its location with `reg.SetEffective` from the configured receiver position.
- `airview admin init` / `airview admin reset --data <dir>` (previously rejected for lack of persistent stores) can now be implemented: open the registry from the data dir in the CLI process and build the service over `reg.Accounts()` / `reg.Sessions()`; SQLite WAL lets this run beside a live server (the CLI must only touch accounts/sessions; the server's in-memory station mirror is not refreshed from other processes).
- Pass `reg.Resolver()` (model.StationResolver) to ingest/tracker/lanes.

## WP-R-registry -> internal/ingest/avfsrv, internal/ingest/beastin, internal/ingest/mlatc (WP-I, WP-B2, WP-B3): admission, claims and connection log
- AVF: after the signature check use `registry.CloneGuard` (Acquire/Release; `CloneRejected` -> WELCOME `cloned_feeder`, `CloneReplace` -> GOODBYE `replaced`), then `reg.Admit(ctx, registry.AdmitRequest{Kind: model.StationKindAVF, FeederPub, RxNo, UUID, EnrolToken, ClockCls})` per RECEIVER; copy `Status`/`Code`/`Reason` into WELCOME; close when `!res.Allowed()`. CLAIM record -> `reg.MintClaim(idx)` -> CLAIM_CODE.
- Compat: `Admit` with `UUID` (`RelaySub` only for TrustedRelays) or, without a UUID, `IdentKey: reg.UnidentifiedKey(ip, 0)`; `PrivacyOff` for mlat-client `privacy:false` (DESIGN Â§19.2 region pin).
- Pending stations feed their own lane only. `reg.ConnOpen(idx, ip, proto)` / `reg.ConnClose(id, bytes)` per session; `reg.Seen(idx, t)` (memory only) for liveness. Per-IP/subnet limits and PerIPStations stay in ingest (raw IPs are memory-only).

## WP-R-registry -> internal/server (WP-H): claim, owner and admin registry routes
- `/api/me/claim`: `reg.RedeemClaim(ownerID, code, s.remoteIP(r))`; compat `reg.ClaimCompat(ownerID, uuid, sameIP, s.remoteIP(r))` (`sameIP` from the live link IPs via `auth.SameClient`). `errors.Is(err, registry.ErrRateLimited)` -> 429 with `(*registry.RateLimitError).RetryAfter`; other registry claim errors have user-safe messages.
- Owner: `reg.OwnerStations`, `reg.Owns`, `SetPrivacy`/`SetName`/`SetDeclared`/`Unclaim`, `GET /api/me/export` -> `reg.ExportOwner(ownerID, w)` (application/zip), `DELETE /api/me/account` -> `reg.DeleteOwner(actor, ownerID, true)` (then clear the cookie).
- Admin: `List(Filter)`, `SetAdmission`, `AssignOwner`, `RevokeFeeder`, `DeleteStation`, `MintToken` (show the secret once), `Tokens`, `RevokeToken`, `AuditLog`, `RunRetention`. Record `reg.Audit(actor, "station.reveal", sid, "")` for every exact-data admin view (Â§8.2).
- Public DTOs use only `reg.PublicPoint(idx)` and `model.StationMeta` - never `registry.Station` (it holds exact coordinates). Owner peer tags: `reg.PeerTag(viewerSID, peerSID, now)`.

## WP-R-registry -> internal/store (WP-D): stations.db coexistence
- internal/registry creates and owns these table names in stations.db: `registry_meta, registry_keys, feeders, stations, station_public, location_estimates, accounts, sessions, claims, enrol_tokens, conn_log, conn_salt, audit, deletions`. Please do not create tables with these names in the stations.db migration subset (DESIGN Â§6.6 lists `stations`, `feeders`, `owners`, `claims`, `enrol_tokens`, `location_estimates`, `station_public`, `audit`, `conn_log` under stations.db; the registry implements them, with `owners` = `accounts` and the owner link as `stations.owner_id`).
- Keep station keys in a column named `ant` (`s<idx>` / `s<idx>/<ch>`), `sid` or `feed` (`st/<sid>/<rx>`): the registry's deletion job removes a deleted station's rows from every other table by these columns. New per-station tables (`station_minute`, `station_clock_minute`, `latency_minute`) should use `t` (Unix s) so `registry.DefaultRetentionRules` applies; otherwise ask WP-R to add a rule.
- Optional: provide an `ExportFunc` (`registry.Options.ExportExtra`) that adds an owner's statistics to the GDPR export.

## WP-R-registry -> internal/mlat/survey, internal/engine (WP-SU, WP-E): self-location writes
Store results with `reg.AddEstimate` (keeps the latest `retention.estimatesPerMethod` per method), `reg.SetEstimated`, and apply the Â§10.1 / Â§19.3 policy (AutoUseSurvey when Ïƒ_h â‰¤ `aggregator.mlat.autoUseMaxSigmaM` and not suspect) with `reg.SetEffective(idx, geo, true, model.LocConfirmed|LocEstimated|...)`. The registry recomputes the public pin and the rural guard itself.

## WP-P-avf -> docs/aggregator/AVF.md (integrator, owner WP0): three spec corrections found while implementing
1. Â§6.3 MLAT_RESULTS: the layout gives **63** bytes per fix without velocity and **75** with (3+8+24+24+4 [+12]); the text says 64/76. The test vector (`85 40 â€¦`, len 0x40 = 1-byte n + 63) agrees with 63. Please fix the sentence; `internal/avf` implements the layout and the vector.
2. Â§5.2 retransmission order is self-contradictory: step 1 sends RECEIVER records with **new** seqs (> held seqs) before step 2 replays held records with seq ackSeq+1.., which the server must treat as a gap (protocol error). Also, replaying only the records "younger than 30 s" leaves a gap when older unacknowledged records precede them. `internal/avf` implements: (a) `SendRing.Resume` replays the held records first, unchanged and gap-free, and the RECEIVER records follow with the next new seqs (the server already knows the receivers from the earlier session of the same seqEpoch, whose watermark it kept); (b) if ackSeq is above the highest seq sent, ackSeq+1 is no longer held, or the oldest unacknowledged record left the resume window, `ResumePlan.NewEpoch` is set: the client closes, starts a new seqEpoch, reconnects immediately and sends the unacknowledged records as backfill. Please amend Â§3.5/Â§5.2 accordingly (or choose another rule and tell WP-P).
3. Â§2.1: Go's TLS server cannot emit `no_application_protocol` when a client offers **no** ALPN; `avf.ServerTLSConfig` aborts the handshake from `GetConfigForClient` instead (generic alert). Clients that offer only other protocols do get `no_application_protocol`. Suggest wording "MUST abort the handshake".

## WP-P-avf -> internal/uplink (WP-U), internal/ingest/avfsrv (WP-I): use internal/avf
The codec, handshake (both sides), TLS helpers, seq/ACK (Sequencer, WatermarkStore, SendRing), keepalive (Conn.RunServerKeepalive, automatic PONG, Conn.Clock), FLOW helpers and Backoff are ready; see `internal/avf/WIRING.md` for the call sequence. Please do not re-implement framing or the signature.

## WP-B2-beastin -> internal/ingest (WP-I): consume the Beast ingest batches and never MLAT them
`internal/ingest/beastin` delivers through `beastin.Sink.HandleBeast(*beastin.Batch)` (contract in `internal/ingest/beastin/WIRING.md` Â§3): frames of one link in order, `Station`/`FeedID` set, clock epoch changes in `Batch.Events` (`At` = index of the first frame of the new epoch). `Batch.MapStatsOnly` is always true: route these frames to the grouper/tracker/lanes, but never to `mlat.Observe`, the clock filter or the survey (DESIGN Â§5.1); `ClockCls`/`Ticks` may feed latency statistics only. A grouper entry whose only timed copies are MapStatsOnly copies must not count as a timed group. Please also expose the global ingest budget as `beastin.Budget` (`Take(n int) bool`) if you implement the Â§4.6 semaphore.

## WP-B2-beastin -> internal/engine, cmd/airview (WP-E): start the 30004 listener in aggregator mode
When `mode == aggregator` and `aggregator.beastPort > 0`: `opt := beastin.OptionsFromConfig(cfg.Aggregator)`, set `opt.ServerKey` (registry's persistent HMAC key), `opt.Now = ntp.Now`, `opt.Logger`, `opt.Gate` (= account service `AdminReady`: listeners stay closed until the admin exists, see the WP-A-auth item above), optional `opt.Budget`; `srv, _ := beastin.New(opt, registryAdapter, ingestSink)`; `go srv.ListenAndServe(ctx)`; `srv.SetPolicy(beastin.PolicyFromConfig(next.Aggregator))` on ApplyConfig (a changed port needs a restart of the listener); `srv.Close()` on shutdown (it waits until every frame was delivered and every station detached). Until internal/ingest exists, `beastin.FrameSinkAdapter{Sink: <tracker or tee>}` works as the sink. Never in standalone mode.

## WP-B2-beastin -> internal/registry (WP-R): implement beastin.Registry
`Attach(ctx, beastin.Identity) (beastin.Attachment, error)` / `Detach(Identity, Attachment)` per `internal/ingest/beastin/WIRING.md` Â§2: key stations by `Identity.Key()` (`uuid:<uuid>`, `uuid:<uuid>/rx:<hex16>` for trusted-relay sub-stations, `ip:<base32>[/<slot>]` for UUID-less links), kind `beast`; apply the admission policy (approved / pending / denied); return `FeedID` (`st/<sid>/<rx>`) and `EpochBase`; implement link_guess (a UUID-less link joined to a station seen on the same `IPKey` within 120 s) if desired; keep `RemoteIP` for the compat claim rule (UUID + same IP) without persisting raw IPs beyond the retention schedule. Provide the persistent HMAC server key used for `Options.ServerKey`. Note: compat feeders cannot present an enrol token, so under `admission: invite` a 30004 UUID needs an admin pre-approval or should be held `pending` â€” please decide and document.

## WP-B2-beastin -> internal/server (WP-H): admin view of the Beast ingest
Admin-only route (e.g. `GET /api/admin/ingest/beast`) returning `srv.Stats()` and `srv.Links()` (`[]beastin.LinkStats`: remote address, UUID, rates, escapes, ping RTT, drops, stations) and `POST .../beast/{link}/disconnect` -> `srv.Disconnect(id)`. `LinkStats` contains private data (IP, UUID): never in public or owner scope; an owner may see only their own stations' rows without `remote`/`ipKey`.

## WP-B2-beastin -> internal/model + internal/config (integrator): file-configurable Beast ingest tunables (Â§20.3)
Optional block `aggregator.beast` mapped onto `beastin.Options`: `identifyWaitMs` (2000, 100..60000), `idleTimeoutSec` (180, 10..3600), `pingIntervalSec` (5, 1..300), `slowDownEverySec` (10, 1..600), `queueBatches` (64, 4..65536), `maxLinks` (4096, 1..100000), `acceptMlatResults` (false), `stripClock` (false), `overLimitSeconds` (5, 1..300), `coolDownSec` (300, 0..86400). The limits already in `aggregator.limits` are used through `beastin.PolicyFromConfig`. Until the block exists the defaults apply.


## WP-T-tracker -> internal/store, internal/analytics (WP-D): flight_station table and writer
`tracker.LaneOptions.OnFlightStation` delivers `[]tracker.FlightStation{FlightID, Station, SID, ICAO, First, Last (Unix s), Msgs, Positions, MaxDistKm, MinDistKm (NaN = station not located), RSSIMin, RSSIMax, RSSIAvg (NaN = none)}` from the lanes' Tick (no locks held; slice reused after the call). Please add the `flight_station(flight_id, sid, first, last, msgs, positions, max_dist_km, min_dist_km, rssi_min, rssi_max, rssi_avg)` migration to airview.db (DESIGN Â§6.4; primary key (flight_id, sid); a second emission for the same key should merge: msgs/positions add, first min, last max, distances and RSSI min/max, rssi_avg msgs-weighted) and a `Batch.UpsertFlightStation(rows []store.FlightStation)` (NaN -> NULL). Rows of unlocated stations carry NaN distances.

## WP-T-tracker -> internal/engine, cmd/airview (WP-E): wire the tracker's aggregator mode
In aggregator mode: `lanes := tracker.NewLanes(tracker.LaneOptions{Resolver: registry, Store: stationsDB, OnFlightStation: func(rows){ b := store.NewBatch(); b.UpsertFlightStation(conv(rows)); airviewDB.Submit(b) }})`, then `tr.SetNetwork(registry, lanes)` before any frame. Give the grouper `tr` as a `tracker.NetHandler`. `tr.Run` drives the lanes' Tick; `tr.Flush` also flushes the lanes. Show `tr.LockStats()` (HoldNs, Utilization) and `lanes.List()` on the admin ingest-load page. Contract: internal/tracker/WIRING.md.

## WP-T-tracker -> internal/ingest (WP-I): grouper contract
First copy: `r := h.Handle(f)` (no tracker-side dedup; it also credits f.Station's lane); cache `r` in the group entry; every later copy: `h.Lanes().Credit(st, ch, sig, &entry.result)` (lock-free w.r.t. the tracker mutex). Late frames: `h.Handle(f)` with `f.Late=true` (routes to `Lanes.Backfill`) - never into the grouper. Never call `HandleFrame` in aggregator mode. For a phase-5 sharded tracker the same `tracker.NetHandler` interface is kept (routing uses `tracker.RouteKey` / `tracker.ShardOf`).

## WP-T-tracker -> internal/server, internal/privacy (WP-H): per-viewer projection
In aggregator mode do not serve `Tracker.Snapshot()` / `Tracker.Aircraft()` on any route: their `feeds` contain namespaced FeedIDs (`st/<sid>/<rx>` = SIDs) and `rssi` is one station's. Use `tr.SnapshotView(tracker.View{Scope, Station: selected StationIdx, Own: owner's StationIdxs})` and `tr.AircraftView(icao, view)`; they strip per scope (public: no geometry, cpa, terrain, rssi, feeds, antennas, mine, stations) and fill `mine` / `stations` / geometry for the selected station (owner: own stations only). Map `model.Viewer.SID` -> StationIdx through the registry. The SSE stream needs one projection per (scope, owner, station) cache key.

## WP-T-tracker -> internal/analytics/locate2* (WP-L2): guess sample hook
`tr.SetGuessSampleHook(func(s []tracker.GuessSample))` delivers {T ms, Station, ICAO, Lat, Lon, AltFt, RSSI, Channel, Antenna, OnGround} for ADS-B positions received off-air, at most one per aircraft per 10 s of data time, about once per second, without locks (slice reused after the call). Write them to `guess_samples` (bounded, 7 days / 2 M rows).

## WP-T-tracker -> internal/model (integrator, model frozen) + internal/config: file-configurable lane settings (Â§20.3)
Optional `aggregator.lanes` block mapped onto `tracker.LaneOptions`: `flushSec` (default 10, 2..60), `slowFlushSec` (default 300, 60..3600), `evictHours` (default 24, 1..720), `maxRangeKm` (default 800, 100..1500; also `tracker.Options.MaxRangeKm`, the aggregator range gate). Until it exists the defaults apply.

## WP-U-uplink -> internal/engine, cmd/airview (WP-E): run the uplink manager in standalone and aggregator modes
Contract: `internal/uplink/WIRING.md`. On start: `m, _ := uplink.NewManager(uplink.Options{Software: "airview", Version: model.Version, StateDir: filepath.Join(dataDir, "uplink"), Source: src, Inject: <tracker sink>, Now: ntp.Now, Logf: ...})`; add `m` to the feed manager's sink tee (`tee{tracker or ingest, hub, m}`; skip it while `!m.Active()` if you like); `feedManager.OnClockEvent(m.HandleClockEvent)`; `feedManager.OnReceiverPosition(func(id string, p feed.ReceiverPosition){ m.HandleGNSS(id, p.Lat, p.Lon, p.AltM, model.AltRefHAE, ntp.Now()) })`; `m.Apply(cfg.Uplinks)` at start and in ApplyConfig; `m.Close()` on shutdown (before the feed manager closes is fine; it spills unsent data to the spool). `src` = `uplink.SourceFuncs{ForwardableFn: feedManager.Forwardable, ReceiverFn: func(id) uplink.ReceiverInfo{...}}` built from the feed config and station settings (stable `RxNo` per feed, name, decoder, channels, `Location` from the station location only when known, antennas, input status from `feedManager.Status()`/`Clock(id)`); never host names or LAN IPs. Results come back through `Inject.HandleUpdate` with FeedID `uplink/<id>`, `MLAT=true`, `Src=mlat-av`; the uplink refuses them on the way out, so the tee may pass them to the manager. Never run uplinks in remote-data/remote-web modes.

## WP-U-uplink -> internal/server (WP-H): uplink status route
Mount `uplinkManager` (an `http.Handler`) at `/api/uplinks` behind admin auth (standalone: the existing admin/settings auth; aggregator: admin scope): `GET` returns `{"feederId","uuid","uplinks":[uplink.Status]}` (no secrets), `POST {"action":"claim","id","rxNo"}` returns a claim code (bearer secret; must never be cached or logged; `Cache-Control: no-store` is set), `POST {"action":"retry","id"}`. Also keep `uplinks[].enrolToken` redacted in `GET /api/config` (config/secrets.go already does this per WP-CF).

## WP-U-uplink -> cmd/airview-feeder, internal/feederui (WP-FB): feeder on top of internal/uplink
`uplink.NewManager(uplink.Options{Software: avf.SoftwareFeeder, StateDir: <data dir>, Source: <feeder receivers>, OnResults: <publish as Beast with the MLAT magic timestamp on 30105>, Inject: nil})`; subcommands: `claim` -> `m.Claim(ctx, id, rxNo)` (print the code, never log it), `new-identity` -> `uplink.NewIdentity(dataDir)` (keeps the UUID), `status` -> `GET` on the status handler. The status page embeds `m.Status()`; state `stopped` with `errorCode` (`enrol_required`, `enrol_invalid`, `revoked`, `cloned_feeder`, `proto_version`) needs a user action, offer "retry" (`m.Retry`). Spool default: `SpoolMB` from config (`model.ResolveSpoolMB` handles the SD-card rule; `uplink.RootOnSD()` is the detector).

## WP-U-uplink -> web/static (WP-W): Uplinks settings section and status
DESIGN Â§12.4: a settings section editing `uplinks[]` (host, port, pin, enrol token as a write-only secret, inputs, sendLocation, shareGNSS, timestamps, returnMLAT, spoolMB, disabled, pin/publicStats/showName) and a status card per uplink from `GET /api/uplinks` (state, error code + text, retry time, approval, mode, MOTD, notices, ring/spool fill, frames sent/late, mlatRefused, results injected, FEEDBACK per receiver), with "Claim code" and "Retry now" buttons (POST actions). Show the consent text (DESIGN Â§10.5) before enabling timestamps for the first time.

## WP-U-uplink -> internal/ingest/avfsrv (WP-I): client behaviour to expect
After WELCOME the uplink renumbers every record the server does not hold from `ackSeq+1` (nothing is replayed with its old seq). Its first records are one RECEIVER per receiver; when queued young data still belongs to an older clock epoch, that RECEIVER announces the older epoch and the queued CLOCK_EVENT(s) follow before the newer epoch's FRAMES. Backfill (inside BACKFILL begin/end, late bit on every frame) may carry FRAMES of epochs never announced in the current session and DECODED / MINUTE_STATS of past minutes; treat those as late data only. GOODBYE `shutting_down` is sent on a graceful stop.

## WP-U-uplink -> internal/model + internal/config (integrator): file-configurable uplink tunables (Â§20.3)
Optional global block `uplink` (or per-uplink fields) mapped onto `uplink.Options`: `ringSec` (600, 60..3600), `ringMB` (12, 1..512), `backfillQueueMB` (24, 1..1024), `resumeWindowSec` (30, 5..300), `backfillMinKBps` (64, 1..100000), `backoffMinSec` (1), `backoffMaxSec` (60, 5..3600), `livenessSec` (30, 5..600), `spoolSegmentMB` (4, 1..64). Until the block exists the defaults apply. Env overrides `AIRVIEW_UPLINK_*` follow the WP-CF scheme.

Servers MUST accept both client strategies: (a) replay held records unchanged, then RECEIVER (internal/avf `SendRing.Resume`); (b) RECEIVER at `ackSeq+1`, then renumbered young records (internal/uplink). The new-seqEpoch rule for an unreplayable gap is included.

## WP-HB-hub -> internal/engine (WP-E): turn on hub admin-only mode in aggregator mode (DESIGN Â§5.4)
`hub.Options` has a new `AdminOnly bool` (plus `ProbeTimeout`, default 2 s, and `QuietLogInterval`, default 1 h; zero = defaults) and `(*hub.Server).SetAdminOnly(bool)` for run-time changes. In `syncHub` (`internal/engine/wiring.go`): create the server with `hub.Options{Port: port, Password: stored.Hub.Password, AdminOnly: eff.mode == model.ModeAggregator, Logger: e.log}`, and on the reconfigure path call `h.SetAdminOnly(eff.mode == model.ModeAggregator)` next to `h.SetPassword(...)`. Standalone keeps `AdminOnly=false` (behaviour unchanged). In admin-only mode an empty hub password refuses every login (open access is never offered), so if the hub is enabled in aggregator mode without a password, please surface a warning (`e.Notify("warn", "Data port: aggregator mode requires a hub password; all logins are refused")`). Optional: expose `h.QuietRejections()` in admin diagnostics.

## WP-HB-hub -> internal/config (integrator): `config check` rule for the hub in aggregator mode (Â§20.3)
When `mode == "aggregator"` and `hub.enabled` is true, `config check` should warn when `hub.password` is empty ("aggregator mode: the data port only accepts the admin with a password; with none set every login is refused"). The probe timeout (2 s) and per-IP log interval (1 h) are fixed design values; if they are ever made file-configurable, map them to `hub.Options.ProbeTimeout` / `QuietLogInterval` (zero = default).

Please have the station deletion (and the owner erasure with `deleteStations`) also run `DELETE FROM flight_station WHERE sid=?` (or the idx key) in `airview.db`, e.g. through a `registry.Options` deletion hook that the engine wires to the store. ## WP-PI-installer -> cmd/airview-feeder (WP-FB): CLI/env contract used by deploy/feeder/pi/install.sh
The Pi installer's systemd unit runs `ExecStart=/opt/airview-feeder/airview-feeder run` with `Environment=AIRVIEW_CONFIG=/etc/airview-feeder/feeder.json`, `AIRVIEW_DATA=/var/lib/airview-feeder`, `AIRVIEW_FEEDER_UPDATER=external`, as user `airview-feeder`, under `ProtectSystem=strict` with only `/var/lib/airview-feeder` writable. Please:
- honour `AIRVIEW_CONFIG` (feeder.json path) and `AIRVIEW_DATA` (identity.key, uuid, spool) when no `-config`/`-data` flag is given (same names as the main app);
- decode feeder.json on top of `model.DefaultFeederConfig()` (the installer writes receivers/uplinks/ui/results/autoUpdate only; uplinkTuning and ntp are left to defaults);
- `airview-feeder status` (run as the service user, env as above) prints the uplink status JSON (`uplink.Status`, with `"state":"connected"` / `"stopped"` + `"errorCode"`), exit 0 while the service runs; `airview-feeder claim` prints the claim code as its first non-empty stdout line; `--version` prints the version on line 1;
- with `AIRVIEW_FEEDER_UPDATER=external` do not swap the binary in-process (it cannot: /opt is read-only for the service); the root timer `airview-feeder-update.timer` -> `/opt/airview-feeder/update.sh` installs updates and rolls back if the service restarts or stops within 60 s;
- /etc/airview-feeder/feeder.json is read-only for the service (0640 root:airview-feeder): settings saved from the status page must go to the data dir (e.g. an override file) or the page must say "edit feeder.json / re-run airview-feeder-setup";
- there is no serial feed type; the installer tells FTDI-Beast owners to run a Beast-to-TCP bridge. If WP-F adds a serial input, the unit needs `PrivateDevices=false` + `DeviceAllow=` and group `dialout` (tell WP-PI).

## WP-PI-installer -> internal/server (owner of the aggregator /feeder/ routes): serve the Pi installer and releases
- `GET /feeder/install.sh`: `deploy/feeder/pi/install.sh` with exactly these lines pre-filled (each occurs once, tab-indented, as `NAME=""`): `PRESET_SERVER="<public host>:<AVFPort>"`, `PRESET_PIN="sha256/<b64 SPKI of the AVF cert>"`, `PRESET_URL="https://<public HTTP base>"`, `PRESET_MANIFEST_KEY="<b64 32-byte Ed25519 release key>"`, optional `PRESET_HTTP_PIN="sha256//<b64>"` when the HTTPS cert is self-signed. Only characters from the validators may be inserted (no quotes, `$`, backslashes). `Content-Type: text/x-shellscript`, `Cache-Control: no-cache`.
- `GET /feeder/manifest.json`, `GET /feeder/manifest.json.sig`, `GET /feeder/dl/<target>` for targets `linux-arm64`, `linux-armv7`, `linux-amd64` (contract below). Also `/feeder/update.sh`-style wrappers are NOT needed (installed by install.sh).

## WP-PI-installer -> internal/selfupdate, deploy/release (WP-UP): release manifest contract parsed by the Pi installer (shell/awk, no jq)
- `manifest.json`: JSON; top-level `"version":"<string>"` before the file list; one **flat** object per artifact (no nested objects inside it) containing the target name as a string value (key `"target"` recommended: `linux-arm64` | `linux-armv7` | `linux-amd64` | `windows-amd64` | ...), `"sha256":"<64 hex>"`, optional `"size":<bytes>`, optional `"url"` (absolute, or a path starting with `/` relative to the base URL; default `/feeder/dl/<target>`).
- `manifest.json.sig`: base64 (standard, padded; trailing newline allowed) of the 64-byte Ed25519 signature over the exact bytes of manifest.json. The public key is distributed as base64 of the raw 32-byte key (`--manifest-key` / `PRESET_MANIFEST_KEY`). Verified with OpenSSL >= 3.0 (`pkeyutl -verify -rawin`); tests: deploy/feeder/pi/pi_test.go TestSignedDownload*.

## WP-E-engine -> internal/store (WP-D): open stations.db for the lane statistics and write flight_station
- The engine passes the lanes a `tracker.LaneStore`. Until internal/store can open `<data>/stations.db` with the station migration subset (DESIGN Â§6.6), the engine gives the lanes the airview.db `*store.Store`; rows are keyed `ant = s<idx>` / `s<idx>/<ch>`, so they never mix with the combined slot. Please provide e.g. `store.OpenStations(dir string, opts store.Options) (*store.Store, error)` (file `stations.db`, coexisting with the registry's tables, see WP-R's note) and tell WP-E; the engine will open it in aggregator mode after the registry and close it after the lanes' final flush.
- `flight_station`: the engine exposes `Engine.SetFlightStationSink(func([]tracker.FlightStation))`; until something is installed the rows are counted (`NetworkStatus().FlightStationRows`) and dropped. When `Batch.UpsertFlightStation` exists, the engine (or the integrator) installs `func(rows){ b := store.NewBatch(); b.UpsertFlightStation(conv(rows)); airviewDB.Submit(b) }`.

## WP-E-engine -> internal/analytics, internal/latency, internal/rfstat (owners / integrator): per-station components
`Engine.Stations()` is the per-station runtime LRU of DESIGN Â§6.5 (cap 32, retired after 30 min without lane activity). Register constructors with `Stations().Register(name, func(model.StationMeta) engine.StationComponent)` (a component needs only `Close()`); `Acquire(meta, now)` builds them lazily for a page that asks. Needed: `analytics.New(store, cfgFn, WithAntPrefix("s<idx>"))`, a latency analyzer for GPS-class stations, rfstat. None is registered yet.

## WP-E-engine -> internal/server (WP-H): engine accessors for the aggregator
- cmd/airview now calls `srv.SetAccountService(eng.Accounts())` and `srv.SetNetwork(server.Network{Registry: eng.Registry(), Beast: <adapter over eng.BeastIngest()>, Links: [server.BeastLinks(adapter)], Uplinks: <adapter over eng.Uplinks()>})` after `srv.Start`. `Uplinks` is set in standalone mode too (empty list until an uplink was configured).
- New engine accessors you may use: `Aggregator()`, `Registry()`, `Accounts()`, `Lanes()`, `HostSID()`, `AdminReady()`, `Stations()`, `NetworkStatus()` (admin-only operator view: listener ports/addresses/errors, ingest core stats, Beast stats, tracker `LockStats`, lane count, flight_station rows). Please expose `NetworkStatus()` on an admin route (e.g. `GET /api/admin/network`) and include `adminReady` in the health endpoint for aggregators.
- `Status().Mode` now reports `aggregator` in aggregator mode (the engine used to report standalone because the mode was not honoured yet).

## WP-E-engine -> internal/ingest/avfsrv (WP-I): constructor the engine can call
The engine will start the AVF/1 listener in aggregator mode when `aggregator.avfPort > 0`, like the Beast listener: bound to `Options.ListenHost` (`-listen-host`), gated on `AdminReady`, registry `reg` (+ `registry.CloneGuard`), sink = the `*ingest.Ingest`, TLS from `aggregator.tlsCert/tlsKey` or the self-signed certificate in `<data>/avf-tls/`, `Now = ntp.Now`. Please provide `avfsrv.New(opts) (*Server, error)`, `Serve(ctx, net.Listener) error`, `SetPolicy`/`Apply(model.Aggregator)`, `Close() error` (waits for the last frames), `Pin() string` (SPKI pin for the admin page) and `LiveLinks() []server.LiveLink`-compatible data, and document them in a WIRING.md. Until then `NetworkStatus().AVF.Note` says the listener is not available.

## WP-E-engine -> internal/model + internal/config (integrator): file-configurable listener bind address (Â§20.3)
`-listen-host` / `$AIRVIEW_LISTEN_HOST` sets the bind address of the aggregator's feeder listeners (default every interface). Please add `aggregator.listenAddr` (string, default "") so it can live in the operator file; the engine will prefer the flag, then the setting.

## WP-E-engine -> deploy/** (installers, Docker, systemd), docs (WP-DOC): command names
- Server start: `airview serve [-data dir] [-config file] [-set p=v]... [-listen-host addr]` (always headless; the plain `airview` with `-headless` keeps working). Unit: `ExecStart=/opt/airview/airview serve`.
- `airview backup [-data dir] [-o file.zip]`: consistent online backup (VACUUM INTO of airview.db and stations.db, config.json, an `airview.jsonc` inside the data directory, `uplink/` without the spool, `avf-tls/`), MANIFEST.json with SHA-256. Safe while running. An operator file outside the data directory (`/etc/airview/airview.jsonc`) is not included.
- `airview restore [-data dir] [-force] file.zip`: refuses while AirView runs on the directory, verifies every checksum first, moves replaced files to `<data>/pre-restore-<UTC time>/`, never deletes. Run it as the service user (`sudo -u airview airview restore ...`).
- `airview version [-json]`, `airview help`. Graceful shutdown on SIGINT/SIGTERM (Windows: Ctrl+C, console close, logoff), bounded by 90 s.
- `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` (command-line section) should list these commands and `-listen-host`.

## WP-FB-feeder -> deploy/feeder/pi (WP-PI): airview-feeder CLI contract for the installer
Reference: `cmd/airview-feeder/README.md`. Write the config with `airview-feeder setup -config /etc/airview-feeder/feeder.json -data /var/lib/airview-feeder -non-interactive --server H:P --pin sha256/... --enrol T --name N --lat .. --lon .. --alt 120m|400ft [--alt-ref msl|hae] --agl 8m --input auto|host:port --privacy hidden|region|cell01 [--no-location] [--no-timestamps]` (Go flags accept `-x` and `--x`; exit 2 = bad option, nothing written). Re-running keeps current values. Post-check: `curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:8099/api/health` (200 when every uplink is connected, 503 otherwise) or `airview-feeder status` (exit 0 connected, 1 not all, 3 not running). Claim code: `airview-feeder claim [-json]`. Uninstall: `airview-feeder uninstall-helper -json` lists the feeder's own files; `-purge -yes` deletes them (refuses while running). Unit: `ExecStart=/opt/airview-feeder/airview-feeder run -config /etc/airview-feeder/feeder.json -data /var/lib/airview-feeder`, `Type=notify` is supported (`NotifyAccess=main`, optional `WatchdogSec=60`), `ExecReload=/bin/kill -HUP $MAINPID`. With `ProtectSystem=strict` the config file is read-only to the service, so saving from the status page fails unless `/etc/airview-feeder` is in `ReadWritePaths` (the page then reports the write error); decide which you want. Default paths without flags: `/etc/airview-feeder/feeder.json` and `/var/lib/airview-feeder` when running as root or when either exists.

## WP-FB-feeder -> cmd/airview-feeder/*_windows.go (WP-WF): hooks for the service and tray
Register subcommands from an `init()` in your `*_windows.go` files: `commands["service"] = command{Summary, Run}` and `commands["tray"] = ...` (signature `func(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int`). The service body is `f, err := startFeeder(runOptions{ConfigFile, DataDir, Environ: os.Environ(), Logf, Logger})` then `f.stop()` on SCM stop (`f.reload()` for paramchange). Defaults on Windows are already `%ProgramData%\AirView Feeder\{feeder.json,...}` (paths.go). `lockDataDir` is a no-op on Windows (lock_other.go); the SCM guarantees one instance. The tray can read `GET http://127.0.0.1:8099/api/status` / `/api/health` and `POST /api/claim` with header `X-AirView-Feeder: 1`, or reuse `feederui.Stats`.

## WP-FB-feeder -> internal/model (integrator, model frozen): results listener bind address
`FeederResults` has only `beastPort`; the feeder binds the MLAT results Beast listener to 127.0.0.1 (safe default). Please add `FeederResults.BindAddr string` (default "127.0.0.1"; "" = loopback) so a VRS/readsb on another LAN host can connect; airview-feeder will honour it via `resultsOut.listenAt`.
